    // Listen for messages from the Flourish embeds to resize the iframes
    window.addEventListener("message", function (event) {
        try {
            let message = JSON.parse(event.data);
            if (message.sender == "Flourish" && message.context == "iframe.resize") {
                let iframe = document.querySelector('iframe[src="' + message.src + '"]');
                if(iframe) {
                    iframe.style.height = message.height + "px";
                }
            }
        } catch (e) {
            return;
        }
    });

    // GA4: listen for newsletter_form_submit from embed iframe and send direct gtag event
    window.addEventListener("message", function (e) {
        if (e.data && e.data.event === "newsletter_form_submit") {
            console.log('newsletter_form_submit', e.data);
            try {
                if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.gtag) {
                    window.gtag('event', 'newsletter_form_submit', {
                        newsletter_list_id: e.data.newsletter_list_id,
                        newsletter_list_name: e.data.newsletter_list_name,
                        subscribe_form_type: e.data.subscribe_form_type,
                        email_hash: e.data.email_hash || ''
                    });
                } else {
                    console.error('GA4 gtag not available for newsletter_form_submit');
                }
                if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.olbgPostHogCapture) {
                    window.olbgPostHogCapture('olbg_newsletter_signup_submit', {
                        newsletter_list_id: e.data.newsletter_list_id,
                        newsletter_list_name: e.data.newsletter_list_name,
                        subscribe_form_type: e.data.subscribe_form_type,
                        email_hash: e.data.email_hash || ''
                    });
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error sending GA4 event newsletter_form_submit', error);
            }
        }
    });

    /**
     * Lazy loading of js
    */
    var jsInjected = [];

    /**
     * Checks if an element is in viewport (even partially)
     *
     * @param element
     */
    function isElementInViewPort(el)
    {
        const bounding = el.getBoundingClientRect();
        return (0 < bounding.top && bounding.top < (window.innerHeight || document.documentElement.clientHeight)) ||
            (0 < bounding.bottom && bounding.bottom < (window.innerHeight || document.documentElement.clientHeight));
    }

    /**
         *
         * @param {*} path path to the script to lazy load
         * @param {*} element element to monitor for entering screen
         * @param {*} minTime force delay manually - to overcome situations where the element is in the first screen and we still want to delay its load
         */
    function asyncLoadJs(path, element, minTime)
    {

        /** We don't really want to check for any element, but just a delayed load */
        if (element === false && typeof minTime !== 'undefined' && minTime > 0) {

            window.addEventListener("load", function ()
            {
                setTimeout(function ()
                {
                    injectJs(path);
                }, minTime);
            }, false);

            return;
        }

        var elementToCheck = null;

        // Helper function to get the element
        function getElement()
        {
            if (!elementToCheck) {
                elementToCheck = document.querySelector(element);
            }
            return elementToCheck;
        }

        var listenerHandler = function ()
        {
            checkAndInject(path, getElement());
        };

        // Replace DOMContentLoaded with a more SvelteKit-friendly approach
        if (document.readyState === 'complete' || document.readyState === 'interactive') {
            setTimeout(() =>
            {
                elementToCheck = getElement();
                if (elementToCheck !== null) {
                    if (!checkAndInject(path, elementToCheck, minTime)) {
                        document.addEventListener('scroll', listenerHandler);
                    }
                }
            }, 0);
        } else {
            document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function ()
            {
                elementToCheck = getElement();
                if (elementToCheck !== null) {
                    if (!checkAndInject(path, elementToCheck, minTime)) {
                        document.addEventListener('scroll', listenerHandler);
                    }
                }
            });
        }

        /**
         * We also want to check in case the height of the document changes
         * (ie ads injected or ads removed based on geo)
         * Note: This part is commented out because it's not supported by IE11 :-         * */
        /*const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(entries => {
            checkAndInject(path, element);
        });
        resizeObserver.observe(document.body);*/

        function checkAndInject(path, element, minTime)
        {

            if (isElementInViewPort(element) && !jsInjected.includes(path)) {

                if (typeof minTime !== 'undefined' && minTime > 0) {

                    setTimeout(function ()
                    {
                        injectJs(path);
                    }, minTime);

                } else {

                    injectJs(path);
                }

                document.removeEventListener('scroll', listenerHandler);
                return true;

            }

            return false;
        }

        function injectJs(path)
        {
            var s = document.createElement('script');
            s.type = 'text/javascript';
            s.async = true;
            s.src = path;
            var x = document.getElementsByTagName('script')[0];
            x.parentNode.insertBefore(s, x);
            jsInjected.push(path);
        }

    }

    asyncLoadJs('https://widget.trustpilot.com/bootstrap/v5/tp.widget.bootstrap.min.js', '.trustpilot-widget', 1000);

<section><div class="container"><!--[--><p class="!my-3 th-info p-2 rounded flex gap-2 items-baseline text-pretty md:pr-5 "><i class="i-ui-info text-lg translate-y-px"></i> <span><!----><b>Experts</b> first:mt-8 sct"><div class="container relative z-10 article-intro pt-3"><div class="content"><!--[0--><h2 class="text-lg md:text-xl" id="about-our-rugby-union-betting-tips-and-predictions">About Our Rugby Union Betting Tips and Predictions</h2><!--]--> <!----><p>This page brings together OLBG's Rugby Union tips and predictions for today's and upcoming matches, along with ante-post picks for the major tournaments. Our tipsters post match winner and handicap selections and explain their reasoning in their comments, so you can judge each tip for yourself.</p><h3>Rugby Union Competitions Our Tipsters Cover</h3><p>Our tipsters post Rugby Union betting tips for the leading club competitions, including the Premiership, United Rugby Championship, Top 14, Super Rugby Pacific, Currie Cup, European Champions Cup and European Challenge Cup. On the international scene, you'll find Six Nations tips and Rugby World Cup predictions, with season-long and ante-post best bets added where our tipsters have them.</p><h3>How to Read the Rugby Union Tips Table</h3><p>Each row in the table above covers one selection. These are the columns:</p><table class="table no-branding" style="width: 100%;"><thead><tr><th scope="col">Column</th><th scope="col">What it shows</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Events</td><td>The match or tournament the tip is for.</td></tr><tr><td>Selection</td><td>The tip itself, such as a team to win or a handicap line.</td></tr><tr><td>Odds</td><td>The bookmaker odds for that selection.</td></tr><tr><td>Tips &amp; Comments</td><td>The share of tips placed on the selection within that market, plus the tipsters' comments explaining their picks.</td></tr><tr><td>Value Rating</td><td>A comparison of the selection's odds with its theoretical probability of winning, based on the percentage of tips for it.</td></tr><tr><td>Add</td><td>Adds the selection to your betslip.</td></tr></tbody></table><p>You can re-order the Rugby Union predictions to suit you by using the sort arrows in the column headings, for example by event, by odds, by the most popular tip or by value rating.</p><p>Once you've chosen your selections, compare bookmakers' odds before you place your bet. Please gamble responsibly and only bet what you can afford to lose, as no tip is guaranteed to win. You must be 18 or over to bet, and free, confidential support is available from <a href="https://www.gamcare.org.uk" rel="noopener" target="_blank">GamCare</a> and <a href="https://www.begambleaware.org" rel="noopener" target="_blank">BeGambleAware</a>.</p><!----></div></div></section><section class="py-2 border-t lg:py-8 first:pt-0 first:mt-8 sct"><div class="container relative z-10 "><div class="content"><!--[0--><h2 class="text-lg md:text-xl" id="how-to-choose-rugby-union-tips-to-back">How to Choose Rugby Union Tips to Back</h2><!--]--> <!----><p>Every Rugby Union tip on this page comes from an OLBG tipster and is recorded, so you can judge a prediction on its merits before you back it. Read the comments alongside each selection to see the reasoning behind the tip, then decide whether you agree with it.</p><p>The tips table gives you more to go on. The percentage of tips on a selection shows how much support it has among our tipsters, and the value rating compares the odds with that level of support to highlight potential value. Look out for tips from our Experts too: these are OLBG tipsters with high strike rates, or strong current-month or six-month profit, in Rugby Union.</p><p>If you would rather start with the tipsters themselves, our <a href="/best-tipsters/Rugby_Union/9">Best Rugby Union Tipsters</a> page ranks them by profit and strike rate, with their available tips alongside. Their stats are tracked over twelve months.</p><!----></div></div></section><section class="py-2 border-t lg:py-8 first:pt-0 first:mt-8 sct"><div class="container relative z-10 "><div class="content"><!--[0--><h2 class="text-lg md:text-xl" id="building-a-rugby-union-accumulator-from-our-tips">Building a Rugby Union Accumulator from Our Tips</h2><!--]--> <!----><p>An accumulator (or acca) combines several selections into one bet, but every leg has to win for it to pay out, so OLBG's tipsters typically back win singles instead.</p><p>If you would still like a fun bet, and only stake what you can afford to lose, you could build a Rugby Union acca from several teams to win in the tips on this page.</p><p>To make this easy, click <strong>Add</strong> next to the tips on this page and the selections go into your betslip. You can also add <a href="/betting-tips/Rugby_League/10">Rugby League tips</a> or <a href="/betting-tips">tips from other sports</a> before you finalise your acca.</p><p>Before you place your bet, compare odds from a range of UK-licensed bookmakers. Our guide to <a href="/bookmakers/articles/best-bookmakers-for-rugby-betting">Rugby betting sites</a> is a good place to start. Then choose your bookmaker from the betslip, and your selections carry over, so you don't need to enter them again.</p><!----></div></div></section><section class="py-2 border-t lg:py-8 first:pt-0 first:mt-8 sct"><div class="container relative z-10 "><div class="content"><!--[0--><h2 class="text-lg md:text-xl" id="rugby-union-betting-stats-premiership-top-14-super-rugby-and-urc">Rugby Union Betting Stats: Premiership, Top 14, Super Rugby and URC</h2><!--]--> <!----><p>These Rugby Union betting stats compare the Premiership, Top 14, Super Rugby Pacific and United Rugby Championship (URC) across the last five completed seasons. For example, a team tipped at +10.5 wins the bet if it wins the match or loses by 10 points or fewer. A team tipped at -8.5 has to win by 9 points or more. The half point means the bet can't be settled as a push.</p></div></li></ul><ul class="flex-1 [&amp;&amp;]:p-0 [&amp;&amp;]:m-0 h-rst-ls"><li data-faq-card="" class="group p-2 px-3 [&amp;&amp;]:mb-3 hover:bg-[#fff6e8] hover:cursor-pointer list-none bg-white border rounded border-c-border-2 shadow-sm faq-crd" data-num="1"><h4 class="p-0 m-0"><button type="button" data-faq-toggle="" aria-expanded="false" class="flex items-center w-full p-0 m-0 text text-left bg-transparent border-0 qa-title"><span class="flex-1 max-md:!text-md max-md:leading-relaxed max-md:pr-10 md:!text-base">How often do favourites win in Rugby Union?</span> <i class="i-ui-dropdown-arrow text-olbg-orange group-[.card-opened]:-rotate-90 text-xs"></i></button></h4> <div class="hidden group-[.card-opened]:block pb-0 mt-2 mb-0 answer"><p>Across the Premiership, Top 14, Super Rugby Pacific and United Rugby Championship, the pre-match favourite won between 72.9% and 75.8% of regular-season matches over the last five completed seasons.
